<script async="" src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=UA-640230-3"></script>
<script>
window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}
gtag('js', new Date());
gtag('config', 'AW-1071761691');

function trackEcommerce() {
    function gaAddTrans(orderID, store, total, tax, shipping, city, state, country, currency, channelInfo) {
        var transaction = {
            id: orderID,
            affiliation: store,
            revenue: total,
            tax: tax,
            shipping: shipping,
            city: city,
            state: state,
            country: country
        };

        if (currency) {
            transaction.currency = currency;
        }

        ga('ecommerce:addTransaction', transaction);
    }
    
    function gaAddItems(orderID, sku, product, variation, price, qty) {
        ga('ecommerce:addItem', {
            id: orderID,
            sku: sku,
            name: product,
            category: variation,
            price: price,
            quantity: qty
        });
    }

    function gaTrackTrans() {
        ga('ecommerce:send');
    }

    function gtagAddTrans(orderID, store, total, tax, shipping, city, state, country, currency, channelInfo) {
        this.transaction = {
            transaction_id: orderID,
            affiliation: store,
            value: total,
            tax: tax,
            shipping: shipping,
            items: []
        };

        if (currency) {
            this.transaction.currency = currency;
        }
    }

    function gtagAddItem(orderID, sku, product, variation, price, qty) {
        this.transaction.items.push({
            id: sku,
            name: product,
            category: variation,
            price: price,
            quantity: qty
        });
    }

    function gtagTrackTrans() {
        gtag('event', 'purchase', this.transaction);
        gtag('event', 'conversion', {
            'send_to': 'AW-1071761691/XZGXCIDKqa4BEJuSh_8D',
            'value': this.transaction.value,
            'transaction_id': this.transaction.transaction_id,
        });
        this.transaction = null;
    }

    if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
        this._addTrans = gtagAddTrans;
        this._addItem = gtagAddItem;
        this._trackTrans = gtagTrackTrans;
    } else if (typeof ga === 'function') {
        this._addTrans = gaAddTrans;
        this._addItem = gaAddItems;
        this._trackTrans = gaTrackTrans;
    }
}

var pageTracker = new trackEcommerce();

</script>

<div class="cat-image"><a href="https://www.rcplanet.com/radio-control/rc-cars-trucks/"> <img alt="R/C Cars &amp; Trucks" class="lazyautosizes lazyloaded" data-sizes="auto" data-src="https://cdn11.bigcommerce.com/s-qkqglu/product_images/uploaded_images/cat-bnr1.jpg" sizes="368px" src="https://cdn11.bigcommerce.com/s-qkqglu/product_images/uploaded_images/cat-bnr1.jpg" title="R/C Cars &amp; Trucks"/></a>

<div class="cat-load">Drones &amp; Racers</div>
<div class="catb-hover">
<h2>Drones &amp; Racers</h2>
<p>Drones are the hottest thing in radio control today. Many of these high tech toys come with state of the art cameras that are great for aerial photographs and videos. FPV Racers are capable of flying over 60 MPH, hurling around tight corners and narrow straight-aways. Check out our selection of multi-rotor vehicles and get into the game.</p>

<div class="cat-load">R/C Helicopters</div>
<div class="catb-hover">
<h2>R/C Helicopters</h2>
<p>Whether you're looking to fly indoors or outdoors, experienced or beginner, you'll find the right heli for the right price at RC Planet. You don't need to be an experienced radio control helicopter pilot to enjoy the thrills that a high quality R/C heli provides.</p>

<div class="cat-image"><a href="https://www.rcplanet.com/radio-control/rc-airplanes/"> <img alt="R/C Airplanes" class="lazyload" data-sizes="auto" data-src="https://cdn11.bigcommerce.com/s-qkqglu/product_images/uploaded_images/cat-bnr4.jpg" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAP///wAAACH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAICRAEAOw==" title="R/C Airplanes"/></a>
<div class="cat-load">R/C Airplanes</div>
<div class="catb-hover">
<h2>R/C Airplanes</h2>
<p>Whether you’re an experience pilot or a park flyer we have the best selection of radio control airplanes from ParkZone, Great Planes, Top Flite, HobbyZone and more.<br/>Not quite ready to hit the skies, hone your skills with our flight simulators by RealFlight. Experience 3D realism and "real life" features with our RF-X Software and InterLink-X Controller.</p>
<a class="shop-btn" href="https://www.rcplanet.com/radio-control/rc-airplanes/">Shop Now</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sml-row">
<div class="col-c">
<div class="cat-image"><a href="https://www.rcplanet.com/radio-control/rc-boats/"> <img alt="R/C Boats" class="lazyload" data-sizes="auto" data-src="https://cdn11.bigcommerce.com/s-qkqglu/product_images/uploaded_images/cat-bnr5.jpg" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAP///wAAACH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAICRAEAOw==" title="R/C Boats"/></a>
<div class="cat-load">R/C Boats</div>
<div class="catb-hover">
<h2>R/C Boats</h2>
<p>Discover the best radio control boats &amp; watercraft at RC Planet. If you're looking for R/C boats, yachts, parts, accessories and more than you’ve come to the right place we carry a huge range of boats to suit all skill levels.</p>
<a class="shop-btn" href="https://www.rcplanet.com/radio-control/rc-boats/">Shop Now</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-c last">
<div class="cat-image"><a href="https://www.rcplanet.com/general-hobbies/"> <img alt="General Hobbies" class="lazyload" data-sizes="auto" data-src="https://cdn11.bigcommerce.com/s-qkqglu/product_images/uploaded_images/cat-bnr6.jpg" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAP///wAAACH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAICRAEAOw==" title="General Hobbies"/></a>
<div class="cat-load">General Hobbies</div>
<div class="catb-hover">
<h2>General Hobbies</h2>
<p>If you're into pinewood derby or model rocketry then RC Planet is the place to be. We carry a nice variety of PineCar, Pine-Pro and Revell blocks as well Estes rocket kits. Kids and adults alike enjoy our selection of educational toys, slot cars, plastic models and railroad sets.</p>
<a class="shop-btn" href="https://www.rcplanet.com/general-hobbies/">Shop Now</a></div>

<script>
    window.onload = function() {
        if(document.getElementsByClassName('productOptionsList').length > 0){    
            var flagforoutofstock = 0;      
            //Get select object
            if(document.getElementsByClassName("form-select")[0]) {
                var objSelect = document.getElementsByClassName("form-select")[0];
                for (var i = 1; i < objSelect.options.length; i++) {
                    objSelect.options[i].text.includes('(Out of stock)');
                    // console.log(objSelect.options[i].text + " " + objSelect.options[i].text.includes('(Out of stock)'));
                    if(!objSelect.options[i].text.includes('(Out of stock)')){
                        flagforoutofstock = 1;
                    }    
                    
                }
                
                if(!flagforoutofstock)
                {
                    // console.log(flagforoutofstock + " " + document.getElementsByClassName("stocks")[0].innerText);
                    document.getElementsByClassName("stocks")[0].textContent = "OUT OF STOCK";
                    document.getElementsByClassName("stocks")[0].className = 'out-stock';
                    //document.getElementsByClassName("stocks").classList.remove('in-stock');
                }
            
            }
        }
    }
    
   
</script>

			var productIds = [];

			var google_tag_params = {
			ecomm_prodid: '',
			ecomm_pagetype: '',
			ecomm_totalvalue: 0
			};
			if('pages/home' == 'pages/home') {
				google_tag_params.ecomm_pagetype = 'home';
			}
			else if('pages/home' == 'pages/search') {
				google_tag_params.ecomm_pagetype = 'searchresults';
			}
			else if('pages/home' == 'pages/category') {
				google_tag_params.ecomm_pagetype = 'category';
			}
			else if('pages/home' == 'pages/cart') {
                google_tag_params = {
                    ecomm_prodid: productIds.length ? productIds : '',
                    ecomm_pagetype: 'cart',
                    ecomm_totalvalue: 0
                };
			} else if('pages/home' == 'pages/order-complete' || 'pages/home' == 'pages/order-confirmation') {
				google_tag_params.ecomm_pagetype = 'purchase';

<script>
function addEventListenerStamped(el, eventName, handler) { 
    if (el.addEventListener) { el.addEventListener(eventName, handler); }
    else { el.attachEvent('on' + eventName, function () { handler.call(el); }); } 
 }
 
addEventListenerStamped(document, 'stamped:reviews:loaded', function() {
    $('.stamped-main-widget-placeholder-main .stamped-header').attr({
        itemprop: 'aggregateRating',
        itemscope: true,
        itemtype: 'https://schema.org/AggregateRating'
    });
    $('.stamped-review').each((i, el) => {
        const $el = $(el);
        $el.attr({
            itemprop: 'review',
            itemscope: true,
            itemtype: 'http://schema.org/Review',
        });

        $el.find('.stamped-review-header-title').attr('itemprop', 'name');
        $el.find('.author').attr('itemprop', 'author');
        $el.find('.created').attr('itemprop', 'datePublished');
        $el.find('.stamped-review-content-body').attr('itemprop', 'description');

        const rating = $el.find('.stamped-starratings').first().find('.stamped-fa-star').length;
        $el.find('.stamped-starratings').attr({
            itemprop: 'reviewRating',
            itemscope: true,
            itemtype: 'http://schema.org/Rating',
        }).append(`<meta itemprop="worstRating" content="1"><meta itemprop="bestRating" content="5"><meta itemprop="ratingValue" content="${rating}">`);
    });
});
</script>
